=== DECISION BOUNDARY ===
PROFILE_+98: classify only if an explicit writer/copy/transform from CM1(+0x40) or another named source is visible above.
PROFILE_+E0: classify only if an explicit writer/copy/transform from CM2(+0x70) or another named source is visible above.
T1/T2 are already proven identical DNG-visible fields because constructor and DNG exporter read the same cm_root offsets +0x38/+0x68.
Do not infer matrix equivalence from adjacency, dimensions, or DNG SDK naming alone.
